# Skills · J · part 25

866 entries, jstack-how to judgment-craft. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **jstack-how**: Use for "how does X work", code walkthroughs before changing something, and placement / ownership / layering questions ("where should this live", "which package owns this", "is this the right layer"). Explains subsystem architecture, runtime flow, onboarding mental models. Use jstack-why for motivation. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-how))
- **jstack-implement**: Implement an explicitly requested software change or approved plan in a local repository, including focused tests and verification. Use when the user asks to implement or fix code; do not use for planning-only work, report-only review, Git history, or remote mutations. ([JustinPrograms/jstack](https://github.com/JustinPrograms/jstack/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack-implement) · ★6)
- **jstack-implement**: Implement one PR from the pr-plan on its own stacked branch with a fresh implementer agent, test first, small commits. Measures size. Then runs $jstack-verify unless --no-verify. ([manhnc-dev/jstack](https://github.com/manhnc-dev/jstack/tree/HEAD/harness/codex/skills/jstack-implement))
- **jstack-implement**: Implement one PR from the pr-plan on its own stacked branch with a fresh implementer agent, test first, small commits. Measures size. Then runs /jstack-verify unless --no-verify. ([manhnc-dev/jstack](https://github.com/manhnc-dev/jstack/tree/HEAD/harness/cursor/skills/jstack-implement))

- **jstack-interrogate**: Use for "interrogate", "adversarial review", "multi-model review", "challenge this", "stress test this code", "find blind spots", or "tear this apart". - **jstack-ketchup**: Catch the user up on what changed in this chat since their last message, in plain words and small visuals. - **jstack-loop**: Run bounded JStack goal loops or durable multi-phase programs until current acceptance evidence succeeds or an auditable stop or wait state is reached. - **jstack-principle-attack-the-premise**: Apply when two or more fixes that share one premise have failed the same gate. Write the premise down and test it directly before you write another fix that assumes it. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-attack-the-premise))
- **jstack-principle-boundary-discipline**: Apply when wiring validation, error handling, or framework adapters. Concentrate guards at system boundaries (CLI, config, network, external APIs); trust internal types and keep business logic in pure functions. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-boundary-discipline))
- **jstack-principle-build-the-lever**: Apply to any non-trivial work, not just bulk work: edits, migrations, analyses, checks. Build the tool that does it or proves it (codemod, script, generator, or a skill your subagents follow) instead of working by hand. The tool is the artifact a reviewer can rerun. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-build-the-lever))
- **jstack-principle-encode-lessons-in-structure**: Apply when you catch yourself writing the same instruction a second time, or notice a recurring correction. Encode the rule as a lint, metadata flag, runtime check, or script instead of more text. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-encode-lessons-in-structure))
- **jstack-principle-exhaust-the-design-space**: Apply when facing a novel UI interaction or architectural decision with no precedent in the codebase. Build 2-3 competing prototypes and compare side by side before committing. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-exhaust-the-design-space))
- **jstack-principle-experience-first**: Apply when product, UX, or feature-scope tradeoffs come up. Choose user delight over implementation convenience; ship fewer polished features over more rough ones. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-experience-first))
- **jstack-principle-explain-the-number**: Apply before you trust, report, or act on a number you measured: a speedup, a regression, a throughput, a latency, or an eval result. Find what limits it, and rule out that it measured something other than the work you think. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-explain-the-number))
- **jstack-principle-fix-root-causes**: Apply when debugging. Trace each symptom back to the first place the system went wrong and fix it there. A guard is a fix only when the state it handles is legitimate. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-fix-root-causes))
- **jstack-principle-foundational-thinking**: Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-foundational-thinking))
- **jstack-principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-guard-the-context-window))
- **jstack-principle-laziness-protocol**: Apply when refactoring, evaluating diff size, or tempted to add abstractions, layers, or signal threading. Bias toward deletion and the smallest change that solves the problem. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-laziness-protocol))
- **jstack-principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-make-operations-idempotent))
- **jstack-principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis**: Apply when introducing a new internal API while old callers still exist. Migrate callers and delete the old API in the same wave instead of preserving compatibility layers. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis))
- **jstack-principle-minimize-reader-load**: Apply when reviewing or shaping code that's hard to trace. Count layers between question and answer, and hidden state in the reader's head; collapse one-caller wrappers and shrink mutable scope. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-minimize-reader-load))
- **jstack-principle-model-the-domain**: Apply when writing stateful logic, or when code branches a lot or repeats a shape assumption across files. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-model-the-domain))
- **jstack-principle-never-block-on-the-human**: Apply when tempted to ask 'should I do X?' on reversible work. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-never-block-on-the-human))
- **jstack-principle-outcome-oriented-execution**: Apply during planned rewrites and migrations with explicit phase boundaries. Converge on the target architecture; don't preserve smooth intermediate states with throwaway compatibility code. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-outcome-oriented-execution))
- **jstack-principle-prove-it-works**: Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. Verify against the real artifact (run the feature, read the actual value, inspect the diff), not a proxy, self-report, or 'it compiles.' ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-prove-it-works))
- **jstack-principle-redesign-from-first-principles**: Apply when integrating a new requirement into an existing design. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-redesign-from-first-principles))
- **jstack-principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state**: Apply when concurrent actors might write to the same file, branch, key, or state object. Eliminate the sharing first; serialize structurally only when one shared writer is a real invariant. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state))
- **jstack-principle-sequence-verifiable-units**: Apply to multi-step work (sweeps, migrations, runs of similar edits) and to how you stack commits and PRs. Break work into small units that each end in a verifiable state, check each before the next, and order delivery so the sequence proves itself to a reviewer. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-sequence-verifiable-units))
- **jstack-principle-subtract-before-you-add**: Apply when sequencing an addition, refactor, or rewrite. Remove dead code, redundant validators, and stub references first, then build on the simpler base. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-subtract-before-you-add))
- **jstack-principle-test-behavior-not-implementation**: Apply when you write, change, or keep a test. Assert what the code's users can observe, so that the test fails when that behavior breaks and keeps passing when only the internals change. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-test-behavior-not-implementation))
- **jstack-principle-type-system-discipline**: Apply when designing types, reviewing a function signature, or writing code in any statically-typed language. Make illegal states unrepresentable, brand semantic primitives, parse external data at boundaries, exhaust variants, derive from authoritative schemas. ([juancamiloqhz/skills](https://github.com/juancamiloqhz/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jstack/jstack-principle-type-system-discipline))
- **jstack-principles**: The eight working principles every jstack agent applies. - **jtc-readable-md**: 人が読むMarkdownドキュメントを、読み手が必要な箇所だけを拾って理解できる形に書くためのスキル。設計メモ、調査レポート、議事録、README、仕様書、提案書、PR説明文をはじめ、人が読むことを想定したMarkdownはすべて対象とする。新規に作るときも、既存のMarkdownに追記・修正・章立ての変更を加えるときも、明示的に頼まれていなくても必ず使うこと。使うかどうか迷ったら使う側に倒すこと。AIが読むことを前提としたファイル（CLAUDE.md、スキル定義ファイルなど）は、明示的に指示された場合を除いて対象外。チャット上で短く答えて終わる場面や、コード・設定ファイルそのものを書く場面では使わない。 ([hisafuru/jtc-skills](https://github.com/hisafuru/jtc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jtc-readable-md))
- **jtc-report-brief**: チャットでの応答を、忙しい相手が短時間で判断できる形にまとめるためのスキル。結論と判断事項を先に出し、詳細を後ろに回すことで、読み手が最小限の時間で必要な情報を得られるようにする。報告・説明・提案・分析・質問への回答など、数文以上になる応答はほぼすべてこの型で書くこと。使うかどうか迷ったら使う側に倒すこと。作業の途中経過を伝えるだけの短い連絡、一言・相槌で終わる単純な回答、ファイルとして書き出すドキュメントでは使わない。 ([hisafuru/jtc-skills](https://github.com/hisafuru/jtc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jtc-report-brief))
- **jtc-report-escalate**: 作業を自分の判断で進めてよいか、ユーザーに相談すべきかを切り分け、相談が必要なときにいつどう伝えるかを定めたスキル。ユーザーから作業を依頼され、着手するときに必ず使うこと。特にjtc-scope-checkでスコープを合意した直後には必ず読み込み、判断が分かれる場面に出会う前に基準を持っておくこと。作業の途中で新たな判断の分かれ目が出てきたときや、完了報告に含めるべき懸念に気づいたときにも使う。使うかどうか迷ったら使う側に倒すこと。 ([hisafuru/jtc-skills](https://github.com/hisafuru/jtc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jtc-report-escalate))
- **jtc-scope-check**: 作業に着手する前に、目的とスコープをユーザーと合意するためのスキル。何を作るのか、どこまでやるのか、どの品質で仕上げるのかを着手前に確定させ、手戻りと的外れな成果物を防ぐ。ユーザーから実装・調査・資料作成など、何らかの成果物を伴う作業を依頼されたときは、着手する前に必ず使うこと。他のスキルが同時に当てはまりそうな場合も、まずこのスキルを最優先で呼び出すこと。使うかどうか迷ったら使う側に倒すこと。1箇所だけの軽微な修正など、やることが一意に決まっていて確認の余地がない作業でだけは使わなくてよい。 ([hisafuru/jtc-skills](https://github.com/hisafuru/jtc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jtc-scope-check))

- **judge-blindness-requirements**: If a judge can see another judge's score, it anchors. If a judge can see the agent's leaderboard position, it's biased toward maintaining rank. - **judgment-board-flow**: owner（判断する人）への問いを、統括（子に作業を任せる親のセッション）ごとに1枚の判断ボードで受け付け、答えを回収して、答えを使う先へ渡すまでの手順。統括が、子から問い・報告・ゲートの停止（plan を書いて止まったなど）が届いたとき、SO（セカンドオピニオン）が終わって採否を owner に聞くものがあるとき、自分が owner に判断・確認・依頼（値を貼る・画面を開くなど）を求めるとき、会話で聞いた問いに owner が答える前に別の入力（子の報告・CI などの知らせ・owner の別の話題）が届いたとき、owner が答えた・「全部推奨」と言った・番号を挙げて答えたとき、答えを子へ渡すとき、統括を交代して判断ボードを引き継ぐときに使う。委譲された子が、統括や owner に判断を求める問いを書くときにも使う。owner への問いをページに載せるときは html-one-pager より先に読み込む（いつ・どのページに・何を載せるかはこのスキルが決め、ページの形は html-one-pager が決める）。答えを子へ送るときは delegate-task の send より先に読み込む（受け取りを確かめるところまでがこのスキルの手順である）。台帳を検証して書き足す ledger.py を持つ。 ([stlwolf/ai-development-hub](https://github.com/stlwolf/ai-development-hub/tree/HEAD/canonical/skills/judgment-board-flow) · ★2)
- **judgment-calibration-wiki**: Retrieve judgment calibration wiki concepts. Use when the question is about judgment calibration: The dual-process frame and bias mechanism; Statistical-reasoning failure modes; The overconfidence family; Calibration and scoring; Structured estimation method; Belief updating as its own skill; Debiasing technique and deliberate practice; Team and aggregation epistemics for judgment; Choice under uncertainty and its distortion. Scan concepts.json in this skill folder. Read only matching notes. Follow basename links hop by hop. Do not load every note. Do not answer from fleeting/ notes. ([chewcorp/rgh-market](https://github.com/chewcorp/rgh-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/rgh-sme/skills/judgment-calibration-wiki) · 2 copies)
- **judgment-case-note-summariser**: Produces a case note from an Indian judgment covering bench, citation, facts, issues, holdings, ratio decidendi, obiter, statutes considered and later treatment where supplied, and maps a set of authorities on one point into a table showing court, bench strength, holding, whether followed, distinguished or overruled, High Court conflicts and binding or persuasive value. Use when a lawyer asks for a case note, judgment summary, headnote or precedent map. Do not use to check whether citations exist; use citation-verifier instead. ([AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1](https://github.com/AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1/tree/HEAD/plugins/dharmabot-legal-skills/skills/judgment-case-note-summariser) · ★1)

- **judgment-coach**: The diagnostic half of judgment training — runs after a judgment-drill session to work out *why* the answers failed, not just that they did. Classifies each miss into a named failure mode, traces it to the reasoning move that produced it, rebuilds the attack angle the user should have run, tracks which errors recur across sessions, and sources real open-source cases where the same error shows up in the wild. Use this automatically at the end of every judgment-drill session, and whenever the user asks to go deeper on a graded answer, wants to know what's structurally wrong with their thinking,… ([orchimada/judgment-drill](https://github.com/orchimada/judgment-drill/tree/HEAD/skills/judgment-coach) · 2 copies)

- **judgment-compilation**: Find every judgment call in a draft prompt and compile it into a form a Haiku-class model can execute: a decision table over countable inputs, an ensemble vote, an evidence artifact, or a mechanical escalation. Use when / Trigger: writing or reviewing ANY prompt text for this marketplace — especially before shipping a SKILL/command/agent containing words like assess, decide, right-size, when it matters, if needed, appropriately, thoroughly, sensibly. ([mwguerra/plugins-haiku](https://github.com/mwguerra/plugins-haiku/tree/HEAD/smith/skills/judgment-compilation) · ★1)
- **judgment-craft**: Select or revalidate a consequential direction while the earliest representative witness can still reverse or materially narrow the choice. First determine whether existing representative evidence already selects among the live courses; only if it does not, examine the real user or system outcome, ordinary failure, and facts that could change the choice, then choose the smallest evidence-producing next step. Use for consequential product, user-flow, architecture, roadmap, scope, or ownership choices; before adding durable technology, tooling, infrastructure, or process; before promoting a tec… ([perhapsspy/judgment-craft](https://github.com/perhapsspy/judgment-craft/tree/HEAD/plugins/judgment-craft/skills/judgment-craft))
